integrates <a href="https://next-intl-docs.vercel.app/" rel="noreferrer noopener" target="_blank">next-intl</a>.</p>
<div class="flex gap-2 my-4 rounded-xl border bg-fd-card p-3 ps-1 text-sm text-fd-card-foreground shadow-md" style="--callout-color:var(--color-fd-info, var(--color-fd-muted))" emoji="💡"><div role="none" class="w-0.5 bg-(--callout-color)/50 rounded-sm"></div><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-info size-5 -me-0.5 fill-(--callout-color) text-fd-card" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="M12 16v-4"></path><path d="M12 8h.01"></path></svg><div class="flex flex-col gap-2 min-w-0 flex-1"><div class="text-fd-muted-foreground prose-no-margin empty:hidden"><p><strong>Why next-intl:</strong> <br/>
next-intl is a lightweight internationalization library for Next.js and comes
with support for both server and client components in the app router. It also
supports automatic language detection and url based language routing.</p></div></div></div>
<h2 class="flex scroll-m-28 flex-row items-center gap-2" id="usage"><a data-card="" href="#usage" class="peer">Usage</a><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link size-3.5 shrink-0 text-fd-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity peer-hover:opacity-100" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></h2>

<h2 class="flex scroll-m-28 flex-row items-center gap-2" id="translations"><a data-card="" href="#translations" class="peer">Translations</a><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link size-3.5 shrink-0 text-fd-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity peer-hover:opacity-100" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></h2>
<p>Translations are scoped by surface. supastarter splits messages into:</p>
<ul>
<li><code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">marketing</code></li>
<li><code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">saas</code></li>
<li><code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">mail</code></li>
<li><code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">shared</code></li>
</ul>
<p>The supported locales are:</p>
<ul>
<li><code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">en</code></li>
<li><code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">de</code></li>
<li><code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">es</code></li>
<li><code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">fr</code></li>
</ul>
<p>Use <code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">getMessagesForLocale(locale, scope)</code> to load the messages for the active surface. Shared messages are merged in, and missing translations fall back to the default locale.</p>
<h3 class="flex scroll-m-28 flex-row items-center gap-2" id="add-a-new-locale"><a data-card="" href="#add-a-new-locale" class="peer">Add a new locale</a><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link size-3.5 shrink-0 text-fd-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity peer-hover:opacity-100" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></h3>
<p>To add a new locale, add the locale to your i18n config and provide message files for the scopes you use in your app.</p>
<p>For example, if you add French, make sure the <code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">marketing</code>, <code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">saas</code>, <code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">mail</code>, and <code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">shared</code> message scopes all contain a <code class="not-prose rounded-sm border border-primary/10 bg-primary/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-normal text-primary [pre&gt;&amp;]:border-0 [pre&gt;&amp;]:bg-transparent [pre&gt;&amp;]:p-0">fr</code> variant when needed.</p>
<h2 class="flex scroll-m-28 flex-row items-center gap-2" id="notes"><a data-card="" href="#notes" class="peer">Notes</a><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link size-3.5 shrink-0 text-fd-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity peer-hover:opacity-100" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></h2>
<p>The marketing app owns locale-aware public routes, while the SaaS app owns locale-aware product routes. Both use the same scoped i18n system.</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 empty:hidden"></div><div class="@container grid gap-4 grid-cols-2"><a
